- **confirm-trip-plan**: Confirms a Baku trip plan: asks for the plan holder's full name, email and phone, validates them, shows the full plan summary, and confirms the plan only after the traveler explicitly agrees, issuing a reference. Use when the traveler wants to continue to confirmation or finalize the plan. ([david-lorenzo88/baku-travel-agent](https://github.com/david-lorenzo88/baku-travel-agent/tree/HEAD/agent/behaviors/confirm-trip-plan) · 2 copies)
- **confirm-understanding**: Clarify a task's intended outcome, scope, and consequential assumptions before dependent work. Use when the user asks you to confirm understanding or improve how agents ask questions and explain decisions; save communication defaults only when requested.
